# Conjectural-propagation sweep — per-conjecture cones

Generated by `pipeline/conjectural-propagation-sweep.ts` (Tier 3 / P3.1).

Per CLAUDE.md §3b, every **provable** block (theorem/proposition/lemma/
corollary) whose transitive `uses[]` cone reaches a `conj:` label must
be demoted to `conjecture`. This file lists the cones grouped by the
upstream conjecture.

**Note (CLAUDE.md §3b clarification):** definitions that transitively
touch a conjecture are *not* automatic violations — only provable
kinds. This report restricts to provable kinds.
